docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3698:docs\stories\E02-S03-technician-kyc.md:1:# Story E02-S03: Technician KYC — DigiLocker Aadhaar consent + PAN OCR via Form Recognizer
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3699:docs\stories\E02-S03-technician-kyc.md:8:> **Ceremony tier:** Foundation (cross-cutting Android + API; PII handling — Aadhaar consent, PAN OCR, Firebase Storage; security-sensitive)
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3700:docs\stories\E02-S03-technician-kyc.md:18:I want a 3-step KYC flow — Aadhaar verification via DigiLocker OAuth2 (consent only, never the full number), PAN photo capture + OCR via Azure Form Recognizer, and a graceful manual-review fallback when either stage fails,
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3701:docs\stories\E02-S03-technician-kyc.md:33:### AC-2 · PAN photo capture + Firebase Storage upload + Form Recognizer OCR
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3702:docs\stories\E02-S03-technician-kyc.md:34:- **Given** the technician captures their PAN card photo on `KycScreen` step 2
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3705:docs\stories\E02-S03-technician-kyc.md:38:- **And** on success, the extracted PAN number is persisted via `technician-repository.upsertKycStatus({ panNumber, panImagePath, kycStatus: 'PAN_DONE' })`
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3707:docs\stories\E02-S03-technician-kyc.md:45:- **And** the technician sees "We'll review your PAN manually — you'll hear back within 24h" on `KycScreen` step 3 (review state)
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3708:docs\stories\E02-S03-technician-kyc.md:50:- **Then** valid values are: `PENDING`, `AADHAAR_DONE`, `PAN_DONE`, `COMPLETE`, `PENDING_MANUAL`, `MANUAL_REVIEW`
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3709:docs\stories\E02-S03-technician-kyc.md:51:- **And** transitions: `PENDING → AADHAAR_DONE` (after AC-1); `AADHAAR_DONE → PAN_DONE` (after AC-2); `PAN_DONE → COMPLETE` (when both verified); `* → MANUAL_REVIEW` (after AC-3 failure)
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3712:docs\stories\E02-S03-technician-kyc.md:72:- **Then** the screen drives a 3-step flow: Aadhaar (Custom Tabs deeplink) → PAN (camera capture + Firebase Storage upload) → Review (status display)
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3714:docs\stories\E02-S03-technician-kyc.md:114:- [x] **WS-B2 — Firebase Storage helper + middleware + Form Recognizer service + PAN OCR (TDD)**
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3716:docs\stories\E02-S03-technician-kyc.md:173:The story is the first to touch user-supplied PII (Aadhaar, PAN). The implementation enforces:
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3717:docs\stories\E02-S03-technician-kyc.md:179:| PAN photo | Firebase Storage path `technicians/<UID>/pan/<timestamp>.jpg`; storage rules scope to technician's own UID (out of scope for this story; tracked in storage-rules ADR) |
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3718:docs\stories\E02-S03-technician-kyc.md:180:| PAN number | Server-side only; persisted to Cosmos under technician document; not exposed to other technicians |
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3719:docs\stories\E02-S03-technician-kyc.md:221:PR #17 merged 2026-04-20 as commit `41e65d8`. PII handling reviewed during Codex pass — masked-Aadhaar persistence pattern locked in for any future Indian-government-doc verification flow. PAN OCR fallback to `MANUAL_REVIEW` (instead of HTTP 500) was a deliberate UX choice — keeps technician unblocked even when Form Recognizer rate-limits.
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3744:docs\adr\0015-kyc-pii-encryption.md:1:# ADR 0015 — KYC PII Encryption at Rest (PAN Number)
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3745:docs\adr\0015-kyc-pii-encryption.md:5:**Closes:** Issue #84 — PAN number persisted in Cosmos in cleartext
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3746:docs\adr\0015-kyc-pii-encryption.md:11:The technician KYC flow extracts PAN numbers via Azure Form Recognizer OCR and stores them in Cosmos DB (`technicians` container, `kyc.panNumber`). Before this ADR, the full cleartext PAN was written to Cosmos, violating DPDP §8 (data minimisation) and standard security practice for Aadhaar-linked identifiers.
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3747:docs\adr\0015-kyc-pii-encryption.md:13:PAN numbers are 10-character alphanumeric identifiers (`AAAAA9999A`). They are used by the Indian Income Tax department and, in combination with Aadhaar, constitute sensitive financial PII subject to DPDP 2023 and RBI data-localisation requirements.
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3748:docs\adr\0015-kyc-pii-encryption.md:19:Apply **AES-256-GCM** symmetric encryption to the cleartext PAN before any Cosmos write.
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3751:docs\adr\0015-kyc-pii-encryption.md:28:The cleartext PAN **never** reaches Cosmos, any log, or any API response.
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3752:docs\adr\0015-kyc-pii-encryption.md:33:- **Key:** 32-byte secret loaded from `COSMOS_PAN_ENCRYPTION_KEY` environment variable (base64-encoded)
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3755:docs\adr\0015-kyc-pii-encryption.md:48:- `GET /v1/users/me/data-export` — DPDP §11 right-to-access: decrypts blob and returns `panDecrypted` field alongside the masked `panNumber`. If decryption fails (key rotation in progress), `panDecrypted` is `null` and the masked value is returned.
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3757:docs\adr\0015-kyc-pii-encryption.md:59:2. Deploy the new value as `COSMOS_PAN_ENCRYPTION_KEY_NEW` alongside the existing `COSMOS_PAN_ENCRYPTION_KEY`.
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3759:docs\adr\0015-kyc-pii-encryption.md:65:4. After migration is verified, set `COSMOS_PAN_ENCRYPTION_KEY` to the new value and remove `COSMOS_PAN_ENCRYPTION_KEY_NEW`.
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3760:docs\adr\0015-kyc-pii-encryption.md:73:- Cleartext PAN never persists in Cosmos, logs, or API responses.
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3761:docs\adr\0015-kyc-pii-encryption.md:76:- DPDP §8 compliance restored. DPDP §11 data export still provides the cleartext PAN (decrypted) to the data principal.
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3762:docs\adr\0015-kyc-pii-encryption.md:80:- Encrypted blob cannot be queried in Cosmos (e.g. "find all technicians with PAN X"). This is not a required query path.
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:3763:docs\adr\0015-kyc-pii-encryption.md:81:- Key loss = data loss for encrypted blobs. Masked `panNumber` remains, but the full PAN is unrecoverable. Treat `COSMOS_PAN_ENCRYPTION_KEY` as a critical secret.

docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:4732:C:\Alok\Business Projects\Urbanclap-dup\.worktrees\E19-S01\api\openapi.json:1307: "kycHardDeleted": {
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:4733:C:\Alok\Business Projects\Urbanclap-dup\.worktrees\E19-S01\api\openapi.json:1323: "kycHardDeleted",
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:4736:The patch has correctness issues around PAN migration and downstream consumers: the migration can delete encrypted PAN data without a hash, and the new storage shape leaves stale or missing PAN data in existing API flows.
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:4740:- [P1] Preserve encrypted PAN when no hash was derived — C:\Alok\Business Projects\Urbanclap-dup\.worktrees\E19-S01\api\scripts\migrate-pan-to-hash.ts:134-135
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:4741:  If `decryptPan` fails during a live run, for example because the migration is started with a missing/wrong rotated key, `derivePanFields` can return `panHash: null` after falling back to a legacy masked `panNumber`; this line still deletes `panNumberEncrypted`, making the raw PAN/hash unrecoverable. Only remove the encrypted blob after successfully deriving a hash, or skip/error those docs.
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:4743:- [P2] Align data export with panMaskedNumber writes — C:\Alok\Business Projects\Urbanclap-dup\.worktrees\E19-S01\api\src\functions\kyc\submit-pan-ocr.ts:45-45
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:4744:  When this endpoint stores a successful OCR result, it now clears legacy `panNumber` and writes the mask only to `panMaskedNumber`, but `assembleUserDataExport` still builds the exported KYC PAN from `techDoc.kyc.panNumber` only. Any technician processed through this path will therefore export `kyc.panNumber: null` despite having a stored masked PAN, so the export path or contract needs to be updated before clearing the legacy field.
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:4746:- [P2] Clear stale PAN fields on invalid manual review — C:\Alok\Business Projects\Urbanclap-dup\.worktrees\E19-S01\api\src\functions\kyc\submit-pan-ocr.ts:54-57
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:4747:  When the new non-canonical PAN guard in `extractPanFromStoragePath` returns `MANUAL_REVIEW`, this branch only updates the image path/status; because `upsertKycStatus` merges with existing KYC, a technician with a previous PAN keeps stale `panMaskedNumber`/`panHash` or legacy PAN fields, and `getKycStatus` can still show the old mask after the new upload was rejected. Preserve the old invalid-PAN behavior by clearing these fields for that rejection case, or propagate a reason so transient manual-review cases can be handled separately.
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:4750:The patch has correctness issues around PAN migration and downstream consumers: the migration can delete encrypted PAN data without a hash, and the new storage shape leaves stale or missing PAN data in existing API flows.
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:4754:- [P1] Preserve encrypted PAN when no hash was derived — C:\Alok\Business Projects\Urbanclap-dup\.worktrees\E19-S01\api\scripts\migrate-pan-to-hash.ts:134-135
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:4755:  If `decryptPan` fails during a live run, for example because the migration is started with a missing/wrong rotated key, `derivePanFields` can return `panHash: null` after falling back to a legacy masked `panNumber`; this line still deletes `panNumberEncrypted`, making the raw PAN/hash unrecoverable. Only remove the encrypted blob after successfully deriving a hash, or skip/error those docs.
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:4757:- [P2] Align data export with panMaskedNumber writes — C:\Alok\Business Projects\Urbanclap-dup\.worktrees\E19-S01\api\src\functions\kyc\submit-pan-ocr.ts:45-45
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:4758:  When this endpoint stores a successful OCR result, it now clears legacy `panNumber` and writes the mask only to `panMaskedNumber`, but `assembleUserDataExport` still builds the exported KYC PAN from `techDoc.kyc.panNumber` only. Any technician processed through this path will therefore export `kyc.panNumber: null` despite having a stored masked PAN, so the export path or contract needs to be updated before clearing the legacy field.
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:4760:- [P2] Clear stale PAN fields on invalid manual review — C:\Alok\Business Projects\Urbanclap-dup\.worktrees\E19-S01\api\src\functions\kyc\submit-pan-ocr.ts:54-57
docs\reviews\codex-e19-s01-20260518-0700.md:4761:  When the new non-canonical PAN guard in `extractPanFromStoragePath` returns `MANUAL_REVIEW`, this branch only updates the image path/status; because `upsertKycStatus` merges with existing KYC, a technician with a previous PAN keeps stale `panMaskedNumber`/`panHash` or legacy PAN fields, and `getKycStatus` can still show the old mask after the new upload was rejected. Preserve the old invalid-PAN behavior by clearing these fields for that rejection case, or propagate a reason so transient manual-review cases can be handled separately.

> # E19-S01 â€” PAN Encryption (Threat I-A1 Mitigation)

**Tier:** Foundation security-sensitive | **Deps:** none | **Threat:** I-A1 (`docs/threat-model.md` Â§A.4)
**Goal:** Eliminate cleartext PAN persistence in Cosmos. DPDP Act compliance gate for launch.

---

## Problem statement

`panNumber` is stored as a plain string on the technician KYC sub-document
(`api/src/schemas/kyc.ts:13`, written by `api/src/cosmos/technician-repository.ts:28-29`,
read by `api/src/services/formRecognizer.service.ts:25`). Cosmos at-rest encryption
protects against disk-level theft but every read by every Function â€” including a
future support-role admin or a stolen connection string â€” exposes the full PAN.

**Status:** `not-yet-mitigated`. **Impact:** DPDP Act sensitive personal data exposure.

---

## Approach (chosen)

**Store only:**
1. `panMaskedNumber` â€” last 4 digits, plaintext (e.g., `XXXXX1234D`) â€” for display + UX
2. `panHash` â€” SHA-256 of the normalized PAN (uppercase, no whitespace) â€” for duplicate-detection / lookup

**Discard the raw PAN immediately after Form Recognizer extraction.**

This avoids Always Encrypted (Cosmos Serverless lacks full CMK support and adds RU
overhead). Hash-based lookup is sufficient for the only existing use case (detecting
a re-submitted PAN). Display only needs the last 4 digits per UPI/banking convention.

**Rejected alternatives:**
- Cosmos Always Encrypted with CMK â€” Serverless tier limits; adds 2-3x RU cost on
  encrypted-column reads; CMK rotation operationally heavy at pilot scale.
- App-layer AES-GCM with KMS-stored key â€” same operational burden; Hash+mask gives
  the same security with simpler code.

---

## Pattern citations

- `api/src/services/sos.service.ts` (post E11-S05b-2) â€” AES-GCM precedent for crypto
  patterns. PAN does NOT need symmetric encryption; hash is one-way and sufficient.
- `docs/adr/0015-kyc-pii-encryption.md` â€” current ADR; this story SUPERSEDES it by
  removing the encryption requirement (hash+mask is the new approach).

---

## Architecture overview

### Before

```
KycDoc {
  technicianId: string;
  panNumber: "ABCDE1234F";     // PII LEAK
  panImageStoragePath: string;
  aadhaarVerified: boolean;
}
```

### After

```
KycDoc {
  technicianId: string;
  panMaskedNumber: "XXXXX1234F";       // last 4 + check letter, plaintext
  panHash: "sha256-hex-of-normalized-pan";  // 64 chars, deterministic
  panImageStoragePath: string;
  aadhaarVerified: boolean;
}
```

### Migration

Backfill script reads existing docs, computes `panMaskedNumber` + `panHash` from
`panNumber`, writes both, then deletes `panNumber`. Run once with a snapshot timestamp
to ensure idempotency.

---

## Work streams

### WS-A: Schema + utility

**Files:**
- `api/src/schemas/kyc.ts` (MODIFY) â€” remove `panNumber`, add `panMaskedNumber` + `panHash`
- `api/src/services/pan.utils.ts` (NEW) â€” `normalizePan(raw): string`, `maskPan(raw): string`, `hashPan(raw): string`
- `api/tests/services/pan.utils.test.ts` (NEW)

**Tests (TDD red first):**
- `normalizePan` strips whitespace + uppercases
- `maskPan` returns `XXXXX1234D` for `ABCDE1234D`
- `hashPan` is deterministic + same input â†’ same hash + different inputs â†’ different hashes
- Schema rejects writes containing the old `panNumber` field (strict mode)

### WS-B: Repository + service migration

**Files:**
- `api/src/cosmos/technician-repository.ts` (MODIFY) â€” `upsertKyc` accepts only the new fields
- `api/src/services/formRecognizer.service.ts` (MODIFY) â€” extract PAN â†’ compute mask+hash â†’ pass to repo â†’ 
discard raw immediately
- `api/src/functions/technicians.ts` (MODIFY) â€” any handler that returned `panNumber` now returns `panMaskedNumber` 
only
- `api/tests/services/formRecognizer.service.test.ts` (MODIFY) â€” assert raw PAN never persists; mask+hash written
- `api/tests/cosmos/technician-repository.test.ts` (MODIFY) â€” assert old `panNumber` rejected

### WS-C: Migration script

**File:** `api/scripts/migrate-pan-to-hash.ts` (NEW)

- Read all KYC docs that still have `panNumber`
- For each: compute `panMaskedNumber` + `panHash`, write both, delete `panNumber`
- Idempotent: skip docs that already have `panHash` and no `panNumber`
- Dry-run mode: report counts without writing
- Logs every write with a redacted summary (technicianId + before/after field names, no values)

**Run order at deploy time:**
1. Deploy this PR to prod (new schema accepts both old + new fields temporarily â€” strict-mode rejection lands in 
WS-D)
2. Run migration script in dry-run; verify count matches expected
3. Run migration for real
4. Verify zero docs still have `panNumber`
5. Deploy WS-D (strict schema) â€” old field becomes a hard reject

### WS-D: Strict-mode lockdown (separate follow-up PR after migration)

- Schema gains `.strict()` to reject writes containing `panNumber`
- Add Semgrep rule banning `panNumber` field name in any new `.ts` file under `api/src/`
- Update `docs/threat-model.md` Â§I-A1 to status `mitigated`
- Update `docs/adr/0015-kyc-pii-encryption.md` with a SUPERSEDED-BY note â†’ ADR-0025 (new)
- New `docs/adr/0025-pan-hash-mask-storage.md` documents the final approach

### WS-E: Smoke gate

```bash
bash tools/pre-codex-smoke-api.sh
```

All 3 steps green before Codex review. Then `bash api/scripts/migrate-pan-to-hash.ts --dry-run`
against a local Cosmos emulator with seed data.

---

## Out of scope

- Aadhaar number storage (handled separately by DigiLocker consent flow â€” only consent
  receipt token is stored, not the raw Aadhaar)
- Always Encrypted CMK approach (rejected per Approach Â§)
- Admin-web display changes (the only place PAN is displayed is admin technician detail;
  WS-B's `panMaskedNumber` projection is a drop-in replacement)
- Migration of historical bookings or other PII (this story only addresses KYC PAN)

---

## Execution order

1. WS-A (schema + utility) â€” TDD red first
2. WS-B (repo + service migration) â€” TDD red first
3. WS-C (migration script) â€” dry-run tested against local Cosmos emulator

The patch changes the PAN API contract without updating existing clients and has migration edge cases that can leak or erase PAN-derived data. These issues should be addressed before the change is considered correct.

Full review comments:

- [P1] Keep PAN OCR response compatible with clients — C:\Alok\Business Projects\Urbanclap-dup\.worktrees\E19-S01\api\src\functions\kyc\submit-pan-ocr.ts:51-51
  When `PAN_DONE` is returned, this drops the existing `panNumber` property. The technician app in `technician-app/.../KycRepositoryImpl.kt` still deserializes `PanOcrResponse.panNumber` and treats `PAN_DONE` with null as `OcrError`, so successful PAN OCR will look failed until the app is updated. Return the masked value under the legacy key during the migration or update the app in this patch.

- [P2] Mask legacy PANs before returning status — C:\Alok\Business Projects\Urbanclap-dup\.worktrees\E19-S01\api\src\functions\kyc\get-kyc-status.ts:39-39
  For legacy KYC docs that have not yet been migrated, the migration script explicitly handles `panNumber` containing a raw canonical PAN; this fallback returns that string unchanged as `panMaskedNumber`. During the deployment/migration window, `GET /v1/kyc/status` can therefore expose the full PAN instead of the new masked value, so the fallback should mask canonical legacy values before returning them.

- [P2] Preserve the existing mask on migration retries — C:\Alok\Business Projects\Urbanclap-dup\.worktrees\E19-S01\api\scripts\migrate-pan-to-hash.ts:81-81
  If a doc starts with a pre-masked `panNumber` plus a corrupt/wrong-key `panNumberEncrypted`, the first live run stores `panMaskedNumber` and keeps the encrypted blob with `panHash: null`; a second run reaches this `no-pan-data` path and overwrites the stored mask with null. That makes the migration not idempotent for exactly the retry case described in the comments.

- [P2] Avoid logging PAN-derived values during migration — C:\Alok\Business Projects\Urbanclap-dup\.worktrees\E19-S01\api\scripts\migrate-pan-to-hash.ts:123-125
  The migration plan calls for redacted write logs with field names only, but this logs each technician's masked PAN and the first eight characters of the deterministic hash. Those PAN-derived values can end up in long-lived application logs during dry runs/live migrations; log only counts, field names, and source instead.
